### 1. Precedence and what is settled

Apply current explicit user instructions and corrections first, then the competition’s actual requirements for compliance, then V5 for developing and advocating the innovation, then this continuation record and its canonical numerical/evidence material. Earlier sample essays are references for writing and exhibit structure; they do not control facts, compliance or maturity claims. The old Master AI Context v 3 is fully read and incorporated here, with its historical source-ZIP delivery contract separated from the current essay task. The full V5 is preserved losslessly in the embedded source archive, with exact size and SHA-256; the readable recovery section contains the most consequential rules without pretending that excerpts replace a complete new-chat read.

Do not restart product discovery or redesign the prototype because a new chat begins. The adopted system is a **cross-provider existing-journey electronics-return coordination service**, funded by institutions, with accepted receivers and accountable handoffs. The first operating area is **UI/Depok**, demonstrating a complete first configuration of the larger service; it is not a decision to make the eventual system campus-only. The submission’s chosen subtheme is **Smart Recovery: Technology & AI Innovation for E-Waste**. The subtheme permits data/technology/traceability innovation; it does not impose a working-AI, field-pilot, PoC or “M3” entry requirement.

Three mechanisms form one adopted argument:

1. Existing-journey matching makes an admissible transport option available without replacing the provider’s original origin, forward-stop order or destination.
2. Institutional payment purchases recurring readiness, coordination, transport/receiving work and exception resolution, including work with no resale proceeds.
3. Accountable handoffs keep the device, its current holder, required decisions and unresolved obligations connected until the agreed next stage is evidenced.

A nearby vehicle with spare room is not sufficient. Device category/condition, owner instruction, receiving capability/time/capacity, offered vehicle capacity, trip timing, additional duration, authorization and funding must all fit before a pickup promise is released. The distinctive contribution is this contextual configuration across providers for electronics returns; reverse logistics, backhauling, constrained pickup/delivery routing and traceability are established concepts. Never claim the invention of reverse logistics, a global first, or an empty market.

The user’s framing is: **“If this idea already exists, here is what it would look like, and here are the steps to get there.”** Sell the complete intended operating arrangement confidently. Do not lead the essay with the prototype’s maturity, internal version, tests or development limitations. Equally, do not convert intended operations into a claim of signed partnerships, achieved recovery or a deployed current system. One precise boundary beside a synthetic comparison or financial exhibit is sufficient; repeated validation warnings are specifically rejected.

### 3. Intended service and actor responsibilities

| Actor | Role/value | What must be agreed or done |
|---|---|---|
| Institution/program buyer | Buys a defined programme for its community: scheduled access, coordination, handoff reconciliation, exception follow-up and reporting. | Community, categories, area, service windows, budget, contacts, payment and acceptance criteria. Campus, office/community host or programme sponsor are possible institution types; the first financial segment is higher education. |
| Device owner | Receives access within programme scope and retains clear choices about device, ownership and data. | Identifies the item/condition, time and intended route; gives the relevant consent; is ready to hand over. Repair-and-return and donation are not automatic sales. |
| Logistics provider | Offers eligible recovery capacity on a journey it already intends to perform. | Existing origin/stops/destination; time and capacity offered; handling capability; additional-duration tolerance; compensation; cancellation/incident duties. |
| Receiving operator | Supplies an accepted destination and competent next-stage handling. | Categories/conditions, capacity, window, fees, inspection, required data/ownership decisions and downstream evidence within its scope. |
| JejakBalik coordinator/operator | Makes the offers usable as one service and maintains the case after assignment. | Checks eligibility, compares assignments, obtains current approval, records handoffs, reconciles work/cost, owns exceptions and reports open obligations. |
| Independent service reviewer/data or handling specialist | Supplies appropriate competence or a separate review where required. | Data methods/evidence, category-specific handling, complaints and disputes; cannot be replaced by a status animation or an AI suggestion. |

The real operation proposed is: commission bounded programme → collect owner/device instructions → obtain provider/receiver offers → calculate and review a feasible plan → authorized approval → owner/carrier handoff → carrier/receiver acceptance → inspection and any further owner/data authorization → evidenced next-stage handling or an explicitly owned open case. A programme is opened only when its promises have transport, receiving capability and a payer. A failed match remains visible and can move to another time or a separately funded dedicated collection.

Opang and local motorbike couriers can be explored for appropriate local categories. This is not permission to use every ojek or every 3 PL for every e-waste condition. Specialist, damaged or hazardous material needs an appropriately equipped/authorized actor and accepted destination. Campus facilities and organizations are candidates, not secured resources. Lintasarta, EwasteRJ, UI units, Gojek, Grab and other named real organizations are not established JejakBalik partners or data providers.

After pickup, cancellation cannot return a device fictitiously to the household or silently change its holder. The current holder remains responsible until a recognized handoff. A full/unavailable receiver, data hold, revoked consent or disputed record produces an accountable follow-up case, with safe handling and a funding decision for new work. A financial service unit can be accepted before the ultimate physical reuse/recycling outcome is complete; the two must not be conflated.

### 5. What current software actually implements

The current workspaces are **Network, Requests, Decisions and Results**, all reading one editable scenario. Selection, camera movement, navigation and animation do not approve a plan or mutate custody. A preview is separate from review and explicit approval. The user-controlled guide follows the same editable state rather than a pre-baked result path.

Canonical domain files: `src/recovery/types.ts` (entities/contracts), `seed.ts` (fictional inputs), `model.ts` (planning/options/approval/mutation/service progression), `persistence.ts` (strict validation/local storage/export). Active interface: `src/recovery/RecoveryApp.tsx`; world assets/views in `RecoveryWorld.tsx`, `world-scene.tsx` and `world-style.css`.

Implemented operations include request search/filter/pagination/intake; ten device categories; editing owner windows, offered recovery capacity, compensation, provider/receiver availability, receiver slots and programme budget; deterministic option comparison/insertion; rejection reasons; route preview; current-revision approval; chronological simulated collection/receipt/completion; data-review hold; retained custody after disruption; validated JSON/CSV/export/import/reload; language/mobile/accessibility alternatives; and actual WebGL/SVG views.

The world is a fixed complete orthogonal grid with axes −36, −24, −12, 0, 12, 24 and 36. One coordinate unit is 0.1 km; distance is Manhattan distance. Speed is 0.4 km/minute. Time is offset from 09:00 WIB. These are authored planning assumptions, not Jakarta/Depok traffic or a geocoded street network. Capacity is **offered recovery capacity**, not a simulation of all forward parcels. Original origins, ordered forward stops and destinations are preserved. Pickup precedes receipt. The insertion algorithm checks consent, handling compatibility, mass/slots, windows, availability, additional-time tolerance, receiving allocation and programme budget. It is deterministic and conservative; it does not claim global optimality, learned traffic or continuous GPS-based rerouting. New work is not inserted after the journey’s departure time.

Current source fixes the receiver per request. It compares journeys to that receiver; it does not globally optimize recipient choice across all processors. Physical custody freezes the existing carrier allocation. A relevant structural or consent change requires fresh approval; imported cached plans cannot execute as approvals. Completed records cannot be silently changed. Invalid imports preserve usable state or recover safely with an explanatory status. Browser storage namespace is `jejak-balik.recovery.v3` and remains local, not a shared organizational audit backend.

Simulated progression chooses the next eligible chronological event. Collection must precede receiving. The software’s `completed` state requires a received item, owner consent and data clearance for a data-bearing device. It does **not** require an uploaded repair, placement, recycling or sanitization certificate. `dataCleared` is a user-recorded review decision; it does not erase a disk, run a method, certify destruction or prove compliance. Repeated advancement does not duplicate events. Import cannot authenticate a real handoff merely because its fictional history is internally valid.

Proposed real operations still require contracts, competent category handling, lawful activity/permissions, real identities/roles, shared reliable records, paired recognized handoffs, incident resolution, actual prices and settlement, data sanitization competence/evidence, downstream evidence and ongoing funds. Those are precisely defined operating work, not reasons to weaken the final-system pitch into repeated validation disclaimers.

### 6. Executed fictional comparisons and interpretation

Actual TypeScript model/seed/types were executed in a scratch-only copy through Node 24 native type stripping, changing only relative import suffixes for ESM resolution. No repository source was edited. `prototype_scenarios.json` records commit, SHA-256 source hashes, per-case category/handling coverage, assignments, rejection codes, baseline coverage/cost and outstanding allowance. Each case begins from the fresh pre-departure seed and changes only the specified condition. Ten accounting/coverage/budget assertions and canonical base-value checks passed.

| Case | Assigned / initial requests | Added km | Service cost Rp | Dedicated baseline served / that case accepted workload | Outstanding handling allocation Rp |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| All providers | 18/18 | 28.8 | 438,700 | 18/18 | 262,500 |
| Lintas only | 11/18 | 28.8 | 187,700 | 11/11 | 82,500 |
| Sela only | 7/18 | 19.2 | 115,900 | 7/7 | 52,500 |
| Angkut only | 5/18 | 0 | 232,000 | 5/5 | 165,000 |
| No Lintas | 12/18 | 19.2 | 347,900 | 12/12 | 217,500 |
| No Sela | 16/18 | 28.8 | 419,700 | 16/16 | 247,500 |
| No Angkut | 13/18 | 28.8 | 206,700 | 13/13 | 97,500 |
| Budget Rp 300,000 | 14/18 | 24 | 287,700 | 14/14 | 157,500 |
| All receiver capacity zero | 0/18 | 0 | 0 | 0/0 | 0 |
| All journeys unavailable | 0/18 | 0 | 0 | 0/0 | 0 |

The base grouped dedicated-collection comparator serves the **same 18 accepted requests**, with 112.8 km of dedicated travel and Rp 662,400. JejakBalik counts **28.8 incremental kilometres above already-planned journeys**, not total vehicle kilometres. Base model service cost Rp 438,700 = provider compensation Rp 176,200 + handling allocation Rp 262,500. Before service advancement all 18 are planned/connected, **zero complete**, 18 obligations open; all handling allocation is outstanding within the model, not an additional cost on top of Rp 438,700.

The dedicated baseline’s **operational** coverage is feasible but its Rp 662,400 exceeds the scenario’s Rp 600,000 budget by **Rp 62,400**. `baselineFeasible` only means it can serve that case’s accepted workload under the modeled operational checks; it is not a budget-affordability test. Zero accepted workload yields true vacuously for 0/0. Never write that both alternatives satisfy all constraints including the same funding ceiling.

18 versus the strongest isolated provider’s 11 shows bounded **coverage from the combined offered capacities and handling capabilities in this authored fixture**. Provider-restricted counts overlap and must not be added. Raw costs/km across cases serving different workloads are not savings. Each baseline is recalculated for that case’s accepted workload, not all 18 if some are rejected. Zero added km does not mean free service. No comparison establishes real partner availability, field savings, annual trip reductions, avoided emissions or a causal market effect.

The difference in the base fixture is 84 km and Rp 223,700, about 74.468% and 33.771% respectively. Those percentages may be shown only with the authored same-workload comparison and cost boundary. Never multiply the 18-device fixture’s savings by yearly volume. A per-100 normalization, if retained, is an index of that fixture rather than a new 100-device route solution or a forecast.

| Structure | Title; opening; evidence-based problem analysis; idea plus why relevant and how implemented; impact; closing. Insight-led headings are permitted if those functions remain. User now wants final section named **Kesimpulan**. |
| Sources | APA7; informative, argumentative and understandable to general readers. Lintasarta facts must come from official fact sheet/official supplied sources. No invented evidence or implied endorsement. |
| AI | Permitted for research, outline and editing; participant’s original idea and factual responsibility remain essential; guidebook says essay must not be entirely AI-generated. This context preserves the author’s decisions and revision comments rather than transferring ownership. |
| Registration | Biodata, valid student-ID evidence, follow `@ui.gogreen`, personal story sharing of official poster with three friends plus `@ui.gogreen` and `@lintasarta.official`; these are external administrative actions, not completed by producing files. |

Registration: https://bemui.link/RegistrationAksiCintaBumi . Submission: https://bemui.link/SubmissionAksiCintaBumi . Organizer may announce amendments via official Instagram and participant WhatsApp group. Copyright stays with participant; submission permits credited organizer/company reuse for programme publicity/documentation/education. Multiple works are permitted if genuinely different viewpoints; work must be original and not published before the competition period.

Base rubric: relevance 25%, originality 15%, evidence/argument 20%, clarity/writing 15%, impact/feasibility 25%. Bonus outside base 100: relevant/accurate/integrated **Lintasarta Peduli Negeri +10**, **#AksiCintaBumi +10**, **Greener Action +10**, eligible published media+10 and personal blog+10; maximum 150. A bonus cannot compensate for weak fundamentals. Greener Action is the DLH BEM UI function covering waste issues, not a claimed JejakBalik partner.

Optional publication must precede organizer submission, include a live link, and be identical to the manuscript submitted to the competition. Editorial acceptance is not guaranteed. Paid placement is not required. The guidebook lists both community-contribution platforms and stricter editorial outlets; those suggestions are not authorization to post or send now. Work on submission quality first.

### 8. User voice, narrative and design preferences

The user’s strongest current correction is structural: **no substantive solution explanation or JejakBalik pitch before Gagasan**. The title may name the project. Build an inverted-triangle opening with 3–6 relevant citations: significance/urgency → Indonesian context → specific unresolved return problem. Then begin problem analysis with the issue-tree insight, link the actual tree and substantiate its branches. Do not replace this with an isolated hypothetical laptop hook followed immediately by product roles; that was the criticized old approach.

Start Gagasan with a plain, self-contained **“JejakBalik adalah…”** paragraph explaining what the service is, whom it serves, who pays/operates and how existing journeys connect to accepted receipt. Judges should understand the actual arrangement with appendices closed. Only then explain contracts, constraints, the worked service and implementation. Insight-led headings/subchapters should aid locating mechanism, financial logic and implementation without turning each framework into a slide section. Final headings are a lead-edit decision; do not preserve generic headings merely because the first builder used them.

Write ambitious, confident, natural Indonesian with transitions that develop a relationship. A paragraph should change the reader’s understanding and let the next paragraph develop its consequence. Do not end every paragraph with an isolated final-sounding verdict. Avoid stacked semicolon/actor specification lists, repetitive “selain itu/oleh karena itu/dengan demikian,” canned key-takeaway boxes, an automatically balanced summary or a generic warning at every step. The user likes longer connected explanation where it earns its space. Do not fake a personal field interview or anecdote to imitate a reference’s human voice.

The main essay supplies the explanation and judgment; appendices carry inspectable details. User originally asked for extremely detailed dossiers, but the 49-comment revision explicitly corrects the first dossier’s **painful readability**: minimize body prose, remove self-description, repeated definitions and duplicate exhibits; let native tables/graphs/diagrams/screenshots/calculations do the work. “No appendix page limit” is not permission to make a second repetitive essay. Protect finance, implementation timeline, risk mitigation and stakeholder responsibilities, but every exhibit should answer a judge’s concrete question.

Sentence-specific appendix references are mandatory: put a visible clickable **Lampiran X** at the claim it supports, not a parenthetical list of several appendices at paragraph end. Use real bookmarks/hyperlinks, black but visibly bold/underlined. The index must link each specific exhibit. Each appendix needs a visible clickable return to relevant essay section and/or index. Make navigation self-evident; delete prose explaining how links work.

The first appendix must be **prototype/demo access QR**, with readable URL. Renumber the existing sequence consistently after inserting it: all main references, headings, figures, index links, returns and cross-references. The exact final renumber map belongs in the lead’s final manifest/addendum; old A–L labels in background files are historical until mapped. Do not silently point the QR to GitHub instead of the owner’s URL. Public release mismatch is recorded above.

Formatting preferences: use RegenCity Grid Indonesia DOCX as the template reference, subject to the guidebook and newer comments. Zero after-paragraph spacing for ordinary prose; consistent first-line indent; do not fake spacing with blank paragraphs. Body 12 pt, appendix tables 10 pt. All text black. Restrained green header fills are now explicitly acceptable and do not conflict with black typography. Narrative table cells should be justified, with meaningful alignment for numbers/short labels; inspect narrow-column word spacing rather than just toggling a global style. No automatic alternating row fills/checkerboards, heavy borders, page banners, slide titles, padded decorative callouts or ornamental card grids.

Default exhibit construction is **native DOCX tables**. Use LaTeX/TikZ for accurate chart/diagram layouts that native tables do poorly (e.g. nested TAM/SAM/SOM circles, branching/flow, distributions and Gantt). Keep meaningful labels authored and editable. Use GPT Image for genuine concept/mockup imagery when it adds value; do not use it for exact charts, numerical tables, or fake actual screenshots. The supplied ReFeel and Dua business-plan PDFs are references for restrained LaTeX/TikZ analytical figures and occasional generated mockups, not templates for turning a competition essay into a pitch deck. Other supplied earlier essays predate V5 and are subordinate to it.

Current palette for document visuals: forest `#346E57`, light sage `#E8EFEA`, optional very light surround `#F5F7F3`, thin rules `#B7C3BA`/neutral `#666666`, text `#000000`. Ordinary table body rows stay white. Prototype identity retains warm light canvas, forest actions, clay emphasis and Plus Jakarta Sans within the app; it does not change the essay’s Times New Roman rule.

The horizontal issue tree must read **goal/problem → multiple subproblems → root causes** with clear left-to-right branching. Separate repair intention from data/privacy where the source categories differ. Available quantitative evidence goes beside the appropriate leaves; do not invent percentages to make every branch look numerically symmetrical. Route/time/capacity are joint constraints, not direct evidence of fragmented UI data. The financing root concerns paying for work when proceeds are insufficient/unavailable; global treatment costs do not measure local willingness to pay. Handoff disconnection is a sourced design inference, not an invented loss rate.

The two supplied issue-tree image examples establish a horizontal layout only. The green example has an oilfield objective/branches with matcha-market leaves copied into it; its labels are inconsistent. Copy none of their content or causal claims.

These IDs refer to the annotated source draft, not final page/paragraph positions. Original matrix status “planned only” belongs to the previous planning turn; execution is now authorized. The lead must append actual completion/verification status rather than assuming the ledger proves implementation.

### 10. Reference-corpus reading receipt and portable lessons

The supplied reference ZIP contains 37 archive entries representing **35 hash-unique files**: 30 PDFs totaling **789 pages present** and five DOCX files. The manifest’s native extraction totals 125,395 words; five PDFs are image-only and required OCR, so that native total is not the total words read. Exact byte duplicates: ID23 = ID17 (Curhatin Yuk), ID33 = ID10 (E-CENG). They were accounted for but not counted twice as independent writing evidence. The same E-Legislation/Grow Together material also occurs in different layouts and in the anthology; different bytes do not make repeated arguments independent stylistic evidence.

The team read all **available substantive contents**, including references, appendices, covers, declarations, similarity-report pages and relevant image-only material. Corpus reviews A/B/C/D plus the scan review contain per-file receipts. These are structural/voice readings, not independent validation of every sample’s external scientific, legal, market or financial claim, nor verification of prizes suggested by filenames. Private identities, student numbers, signatures and biodata are not reproduced here and are not useful evidence for JejakBalik.

#### 10.1 Exact unavailability limits and what to do with them now

- **ID20 original cutoff:** actual final PDF page 51 carries printed page 79 and ends mid-paragraph in **“Setara Mobile App: Comprehensive HIV Services for Men Who Have Sex with Men and Transgender Women during COVID-19 Pandemic in Indonesia”** with “Most of the public”. The six prior essays are complete. Exact-title/author/anthology searches and author distribution pages, Henbuk preview, Google Books/Play listings and a related author article confirmed the full collection’s identity but did not expose the missing continuation. Full edition is paid; no purchase/login/outreach occurred. It is incorrect to claim the complete original anthology was read.
- **ID20 GoDiet assets:** broken/empty mockup frames physical p. 37 and absent recoverable interface slots/captions pp 38–41 are a supplied-file problem. Surrounding prose/captions were read; successful mockups were not invented.
- **ID25 GINTECH gap:** printed pagination jumps 7→11; printedpages 8–10, apparently Appendices 1–2, are absent. Two search engines and local duplicate checking found no accessible complete original. All 14 present pages were read; highlighted sections recovered by second-pass OCR. The user cannot provide missing originals, so proceed with available material and preserve this bounded receipt.
- **Small pixels:** SOLVAEROPHIL p 21 code collage (approximately 461–464×275–276 per frame), ADRODECO code, SAKARA dense cells, several phone cards and administrative screenshots cannot be transcribed perfectly. Their substantive surrounding prose/principal labels/argumentative role were covered. No source code, every UI field or microscopic cell is claimed audited. No current JejakBalik decision depends on those unread microcharacters.
- Kontura’s absent referenced app appendix and E-Legislation’s empty appendix headings are source-internal omissions, not a retrieval failure. Do not invent their promised content.

Do not stall or re-ask the user for these unavailable originals. They are writing references, not facts to be reused. The remaining material is sufficient for the authorized revision, provided the receipt says **all available substantive material**, not all complete originals.

#### 10.2 The anthology inside ID32

Physical page map: cover 1; E-Legislation first 2–10; RGSH11–25; Leader Lens 26–36; Grow Together 37–51; Smart Investment House 52–63; Eventlink 64–77; Desa Cakap Agraris 78–89; Citizen Charter 90–101; E-Legislation formal variant 102–113; Youth Movement in Politics 114–123. All 3,805 text lines were read in ten nontruncated sequential chunks and 45 image/diagram/layout pages visually inspected; key roadmaps/surveys/interfaces enlarged.

Its best lessons: Eventlink explains offers, availability, selection and the coordinator’s action when a participant is passive; Citizen Charter turns aspiration into time/cost/responsibility/complaint obligations; Desa Cakap Agraris narrows policy scale to a specific local bottleneck; RGSH follows the same beneficiary from blocked access to referral; Grow Together maps causes to different responses. Its defects must not transfer: normative preambles, repeated feature descriptions, unsupported “has succeeded” tense, generated illegible diagram text, and a Leader Lens 30% body versus 40% appendix mismatch. The same subject or obligation should carry a paragraph into the next; extra conjunctions alone do not create flow.

#### 10.3 Selected models, not whole-document templates

Use SAKARA for the breadth of an operating/business case, SIM-Sehat and Integrasi Tiga Titik for a connected service/transaction, Jakarta Smart Island for following a flow into capacity/resources/implementation, Eventlink for actor coordination, Citizen Charter/E-Legislation for institutional response, ADRODECO/SOLVAEROPHIL for precise sentence-level evidence calls, and Nostalgia/Nexus for authored cadence and judgment. References do not authorize unsupported efficacy, a first-ever claim, borrowed surveys/partnerships or a catalogue of frameworks. V5 overrides weak habits in all of them.

### 11. Canonical financial model: assumptions, equations and results

Authoritative machine records are `finance_model.py`, `finance.json`, `finance_tables.json`, `finance_mc_draws.csv` and `method_appendix.json`, originally under production and copied unchanged into the revision workspace where appropriate. The financial analyst confirmed the canonical finance files are unchanged for the new revision. This section preserves their substantive contents so a future chat is not dependent on those paths existing.

All figures are **Estimasi Tim**, **constant 2027 rupiah**, 36 months January 2027–December 2029, before tax/interest/owner distribution. They are not operator quotes, approved budgets, secured contracts or a field forecast. Model adoption may use fractional **equivalent contracted institution-months** in scenarios; it does not assert fractional real institutions/devices. Base whole counts are integers. Display expected device counts rounded for prose while retaining exact values for calculations.

#### 11.1 Unit service, prices and costs

The revenue unit is **one device accepted at its destination with handoff reconciled**, not pickup alone, material recovery, completed recycling, repaired placement or avoided landfill. Monthly institution retainer Rp 3,000,000 is paid in advance; category device service is recognized on receipt/reconciliation and cash arrives one month later in base. Resale/device/material proceeds, carbon credits, terminal value, automatic grants/loans and future uncontracted sponsorship are **zero** in base revenue.

| Category | Mix | Fee/device | Transport | Receiver/handling | Review | Exception allowance | Direct cost/device |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Ordinary/biasa | 80% | 45,000 | 15,000 | 12,000 | 2,000 | 2,000 | 31,000 |
| Bulky/besar | 15% | 110,000 | 45,000 | 30,000 | 3,000 | 4,000 | 82,000 |
| Specialist/khusus | 5% | 190,000 | 60,000 | 70,000 | 5,000 | 10,000 | 145,000 |

Weighted fee Rp 62,000/device; weighted direct cost Rp 44,350; unit contribution Rp 17,650 before shared costs. At 40 devices/institution/month: revenue Rp 5,480,000, direct cost Rp 1,774,000, contribution Rp 3,706,000 before shared work. Exception allowances are assumed spent in direct cost; do not add them a second time. Specialist services require compatible/authorized actors. These business unit costs are deliberately **not calibrated from** the 18-device prototype’s Rp 438,700 fixture.

Shared costs: management 150 hours/month×Rp 40,000 = Rp 6 m; operations 50 hours×Rp 40,000 = Rp 2 m per block, one block per ceiling(active institutions/4), minimum one. Other shared costs totalRp 2.1 m/month: hosting 350 k, workspace 500 k, communications 250 k, professional review 500 k, protection/incident administration 250 k, coordination travel 250 k. Protection is an administrative provision, not a proven insurance policy. Marketing is Rp 1 m/month in 2027, Rp 1.2 m in 2028, Rp 1.5 m in 2029. Onboarding Rp 500 k per additional institution. Shared first-year starting cost is Rp 11.1 m/month, including paid people from preparation onward.

Setup outlay Rp 28 m at month 0: equipmentRp 12 m and software/service setup/trainingRp 16 m. It is separate from operating costs; no terminal equipment salvage is booked. Workload requirement =80 shared hours/month+8 hours/institution+0.2 hours/device. Paid capacity =150 management hours+50×max(1,ceil(institutions/4)). Every base/scenario/Monte Carlo run asserts required hours≤paid capacity; this says staff-time is funded, not that partner trip/receiver capacity is unlimited.

#### 11.2 Time, volumes and operating milestones

Base active institutions months 1–12: **0,0,1,1,2,2,3,3,3,4,4,4**. Core contracts, trained staff and shared records ready by end February; first service **March/month 3**. Additional first-year institutions start months 5,7,10. In 2028, each quarter uses 5,6,7,8 institutions; in 2029 uses 9,10,11,12. Closing counts 4/8/12 are different from institution-months 27/78/126. Do not multiply the closing count by 12 when calculating annual revenue.

Gantt consistency is mandatory: readiness months 1–2, training month 2, initial service begins 3, reconciliation begins 3 and continues 12, later programmes start 5. The prior actual embedded Gantt extended readiness to 3 and reconciliation to 4 despite revenue in 3; that was fixed in the previous review and must not regress in the revision. Ten-year strategic horizon 2027–2036 stages first operation, multiple institutional programmes, wider Jabodetabek capability, new-area preparation and replication. It is a capability direction, not a ten-year financed contract forecast.

| Base annual item | 2027 | 2028 | 2029 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Institution-months | 27 | 78 | 126 |

| Devices received/reconciled | 1,080 | 3,120 | 5,040 |
| Service revenue | 147,960,000 | 427,440,000 | 690,480,000 |

| Shared costs | 133,200,000 | 159,600,000 | 187,200,000 |

Three-year base totals: 9,240 devices; revenue Rp 1,265,880,000; operating costs Rp 895,794,000. Setup is not included again in operating cost. Base NPV **Rp 224,666,633.315804**, cash payback month 22, required funding **Rp 104,816,500**, minimum financed cash Rp 28,714,000, minimum headroom above reserve Rp 5,183,500. Planned basic financing Rp 110 m, actual committed funds **zero**. Proposed contingent envelope Rp 50 m gives total planning ceiling Rp 160 m; it is not automatic money or a guaranteed maximum-loss limit.

Steady operating break-even at starting shared cost Rp 11.1 m is ceil(11.1 m/3.706 m)=**three institutions** at 40 devices each, without new onboarding. It is extremely close: required retainer at three is Rp 2.994 m/institution/month, versus plan Rp 3 m. Four institutions give more room; required retainer at fourRp 2.069 m under the same initial shared cost. At three institutions, about 118.98017 devices/month are needed at Rp 3 m retainers. Confirmation of three consecutive positive operating surplus months ismonth 10 (months 8–10), distinct from cash payback 22.

#### 11.3 Equations and cash definitions

Let t=1…36, h⁰ the base institution schedule, and factors a,v,r,f,d,o change contracted volume, per-institution device volume, retainer, device fee, direct cost and shared cost. I=Rp 28 m, rate 12% effective annual real, ℓ=device cash lag, p=current-month retainer prepayment share. Before month 1, flows are zero.

- h_t=h⁰_t×a; q_t=40×h_t×v.
- T_t=3,000,000×h_t×r; U_t=62,000×q_t×f; revenueY_t=T_t+U_t.
- DirectD_t=44,350×q_t×d.
- SharedB_t=[6,000,000+2,000,000×max(1,ceil(h_t/4))+2,100,000+marketing_t]×o.
- Onboarding A_t=500,000×max(0,h_t−h_(t−1)); expense E_t=D_t+B_t+A_t; operating surplus=Y_t−E_t.
- Cash in K_t=U_(t−ℓ)+pT_t+(1−p)T_(t−1); net cash CF_t=K_t−E_t.
- C_0=−I; C_t=−I+ΣCF_s; financed cash=110,000,000+C_t. Receivables=Σ(Y_s−K_s).
- Reserve Z_t=2 B_t+D_t/4: two months shared cost plus one week direct service cost. Reserve is a **cash floor, not a duplicate expense**.
- Required funding F*=max[0,I+Z_1,max_t(Z_t−C_t)]. Funding contributions are not revenue.
- NPV=−I+Σ_(t=1)^36 CF_t/(1.12)^(t/12). Effective monthly rate=(1.12)^(1/12)−1. No financing flow or terminal liquidation is included; remaining receivables/cash are not invented terminal sale proceeds.
- Payback is the first month with C_t≥0 **and all later C_s≥0** through 36. If none, no payback within horizon.
- Year 3 cost coverage=Σ_(25…36)Y_t/Σ_(25…36)E_t. It is a period operating ratio, not NPV or cash-liquidity proof.

#### 11.4 Deterministic scenarios and sensitivity

Downside: a 0.65, v 0.75, r 0.80, d 1.20, ℓ2 months, p 0.80; other factors 1. Upside: a 1.10, v 1.10, r 1.05, d 0.95, ℓ1, p 1. Scenarios share the same 36-month model and staff-block logic. Fractional institution equivalents do not become asserted real client counts.

| Three-year item | Downside | Base | Upside |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Expected devices | 4,504.5 | 9,240 | 11,180.4 |
| Revenue Rp | 639,639,000 | 1,265,880,000 | 1,493,599,800 |
| Operating costRp | 687,629,490 | 895,794,000 | 981,658,203 |
| NPV Rp | −111,497,642.129953 | 224,666,633.315804 | 332,644,447.347751 |
| Required fundingRp | 180,863,087.5 | 104,816,500 | 100,242,526.75 |
| Cash payback month | Not within 36 | 22 | 19 |

Downside cannot be erased by calling the base investable; its funding requirement exceeds even the 160 m proposed envelope. Response: defer new commitments, renegotiate payment/scope, combine eligible service or establish additional real funding before making new promises. Preserve existing device obligations. One-factor sensitivities change device volume±20%, contracted months±20%, retainer±15%, direct cost±20%, shared cost±15%, or device cash lag to 2 months. Staff cost steps mean some effects are nonlinear.

#### 11.5 Monte Carlo, exact method and interpretation

**10,000 draws**, NumPy `default_rng`/PCG64, seed **20261104**. A primitive set is drawn independently once per iteration, and its derived factors apply to the **whole 36 months**, not a new draw monthly. Triangular T(min,mode,max):

| Primitive | Distribution |
|---|---|
| Participation P | T(0.55,1,1.15) |
| Specific volume V | T(0.65,1,1.15) |
| Retainer pricing R | T(0.80,1,1.10) |
| Direct cost D | T(0.90,1,1.30) |
| Specific overhead O | T(0.90,1,1.20) |
| Device fee F | T(0.90,1,1.08) |
| Cash lagℓ | 0/1/2 months with weights 0.10/0.65/0.25 |
| Prepaid share p | T(0.70,1,1) |

Draw order P,V,R,D,O,F,ℓ,p matters for exact reproducibility. Derived: a=clip[P×(1−0.25×(R−1)),0.45,1.25]; v=0.6 P+0.4 V; r=R; f=F; d=D; o=0.5 D+0.5 O. Thus contracts and volume share participation, cost factors share direct cost, and higher accepted retainer mildly reduces acquisition. Do not present all revenue inputs as independently stacking upside. No distribution was calibrated from JejakBalik field transactions.

| MC metric | P10 | P50 | P90 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| NPV Rp | −21,187,409.320213 | 94,659,302.536999 | 208,330,821.586089 |
| Required fundingRp | 109,205,936.558502 | 128,358,939.492195 | 155,631,321.400158 |
| Year 3 cost coverage | 1.328293 | 1.491806 | 1.655386 |
| Closing unfinanced cash Rp | 5,569,538.750937 | 150,790,393.470264 | 292,619,822.609414 |

Frequency NPV≥0=**84.74%**; year 3 coverage≥1=100% **within these chosen distributions**;110 m funding covers the required cash floor in **11.3%** of draws;160 m covers threshold in **92.95%**. These are conditional simulation frequencies, not probabilities of market success, guaranteed solvency or prediction intervals. P90 funding is not a maximum possible loss. The 160 m envelope remains proposed, committed funds 0.

Quantiles use NumPy linear percentiles. Numerical stability compares first 5,000 draws with 10,000: maximum absolute change among P10/P50/P90  divided by full P90−P10. Ratios: NPV0.007073, funding 0.003186, year 3 coverage 0.007425, closingcash 0.008287 (all under 1%). This checks numerical stability only. Spearman uses Pearson correlation of ranks with tied rank averages; no causal effect implied. Associations with NPV: participation 0.793253, volume 0.710639, retainer 0.260280, direct cost−0.438010, shared cost−0.442250, device fee 0.160213, cashlag−0.139776, prepayment 0.020834.

#### 11.6 TAM/SAM/SOM

Official 2025 statistics: **4,303 higher-education institutions across ministries**. This is an institutional universe, not verified paying demand, all campuses/offices or a regional list. Team reference package=12×(3 m+40×62 k)=**Rp 65.76 m/year**.

- TAM=4,303×65.76 m=**Rp 282,965,280,000/year** service-equivalent envelope.
- SAM=**100 programmes**, team-assumed offer coverage in Jabodetabek, not an official verified regional count;100×65.76 m=**Rp 6,576,000,000/year**.
- SOM2029=**126 institution-months×Rp 5.48 m=Rp 690.48 m**,5,040 devices,12 institutions at year end. Do not use 12×12 months because growth occurs during the year.

The nested circle graphic is schematic, not area-proportional measured market share. Institution eligibility, budgets and purchase choices remain assumptions to be resolved by the implementation process, not presumed approval.

### 12. New quantitative-impact module: preserve its exact meaning

The adopted quantitative-impact extension is `revision_v2/impact.py`, `impact.json`, `impact_tables.json` and `impact_recommendation.md`. It leaves the financial model unchanged and derives service volumes from the same downside/base/upside factors. The chosen main-text case is **9,240 received/reconciled devices over 2027–2029**, with a category-weight range of **29.6–82.7 tonnes entering documented receiving channels** at that same base volume. This is not actual recycling or material recovered. The paired volume-plus-weight matrix belongs in the appendix only. New mass averages remain explicit team assumptions, not externally measured facts.

The user explicitly wants the **magnitude and practical meaning of expected contribution**, not a Dampak section consisting only of 95% timeliness or other quality KPIs. Use conditional team planning quantities and a meaningful range. Separate received devices, reuse/repair, recovered materials and eventual treatment; no recycling yield or reuse-placement rate is established. No CO2-equivalent coefficient is adopted. No annual journey savings are inferred by multiplying the 18-device fixture.

#### 12.1 Volume and funded work

Volume multipliers relative to base are downside 0.65×0.75=0.4875, base 1, upside 1.10×1.10=1.21. Low/high are deterministic scenarios, not confidence bounds or fitted probabilities.

| Service contribution | Low/downside | Base | High/upside |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Devices received/reconciled 2027 | 526.5 | 1,080 | 1,306.8 |
| Devices received/reconciled 2028 | 1,521 | 3,120 | 3,775.2 |
| Devices received/reconciled 2029 | 2,457 | 5,040 | 6,098.4 |
| Devices received/reconciled,2027–29 | 4,504.5 | 9,240 | 11,180.4 |
| Funded management+operations hours,2027 | 2,400 | 2,400 | 2,550 |
| Funded management+operations hours,2028 | 2,700 | 3,000 | 3,150 |
| Funded management+operations hours,2029 | 3,000 | 3,600 | 3,900 |
| Funded management+operations hours,total | 8,100 | 9,000 | 9,600 |
| Estimated required work hours,total | 4,982.1 | 6,576 | 7,148.88 |

These are paid **capacity hours**, including preparation/readiness, not jobs created, guaranteed hours used or additional national employment. Unknown carrier/receiver working hours are not added. At base, transport-service budget over three years is **Rp 200.97 m** and receiving/handling budget **Rp 162.624 m**, both already inside direct costs. They are purchased work, not incremental social surplus or extra company profit. Minimum pickup+receiving event counts are twice the received cohort if every device has those two stages; they are not twice as many devices or beneficiaries.

#### 12.2 New category-weight assumptions and mass sensitivity

The analyst added explicit **Estimasi Tim category-average weights**, not sourced average weights for Indonesian e-waste or hard minimum/maximum weights of individual items. They use the same 80/15/5 service mix:

| Category | Low kg average | Base kg average | High kg average |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Ordinary | 1 | 2 | 4 |
| Bulky | 15 | 25 | 35 |
| Specialist | 3 | 6 | 10 |
| Weighted programme average | **3.20** | **5.65** | **8.95** |

Ordinary is a mix of smaller IT/laptops/peripherals; bulky a mix of monitors/printers/larger appliances; specialist a mix including batteries/UPS or other specifically handled items. These broad categories are not the same distribution as the 18-device fixture. They do not imply every phone weighs 1 kg or every individual battery 6 kg. No cited source in the six-source opening pack supplies a transferable UI/Depok average or recovery yield. A future operator would weigh actual cohorts; until then the mass calculation is explicitly a team-assumption sensitivity, and the adopted main essay mass range must retain that basis.

At the adopted base volume of 9,240 devices, mass entering documented receiving channels is **29.568–82.698 tonnes**, presented in the essay as **29.6–82.7 tonnes**, across low/high weight assumptions. The base-weight point is **52.206 tonnes**; it is an appendix reconstruction value rather than a separate observed result. At base volume, annual base weight mass is 6.102 t/17.628 t/28.476 t in 2027/28/29. Combining low volume+low weight to high volume+high weight gives 14.4144–100.06458 t over three years; this wider matrix belongs in sensitivity, not a clean single observed impact headline.

| Three-year expected devices | Low weight 3.20 kg | Base weight 5.65 kg | High weight 8.95 kg |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Low 4,504.5 | 14.4144 t | 25.450425 t | 40.315275 t |
| Base 9,240 | 29.568 t | 52.206 t | 82.698 t |
| High 11,180.4 | 35.77728 t | 63.16926 t | 100.06458 t |

The quantity is **mass received into a documented service channel**. It is not material recovered, recycling completed, net diversion from landfill, net waste prevented, extra useful-life years or avoided emissions. Respect the user’s desired confidence by explaining the intended contribution clearly and placing the concise assumption next to its calculation, not by removing that distinction.

The module also normalizes the 18-device logistics fixture to an index per 100 equivalent returns:160 additional shared km versus 626.666667 dedicated km, service cost Rp 2,437,222.222222 versus Rp 3,680,000, differences 466.666667 km and Rp 1,242,777.777778. This is **only normalization of the same synthetic fixture**, not a newly optimized 100-device route, annual forecast or CO2 conversion. Keep it in a calculation exhibit if useful; no annual extrapolation is supported.

### 13. Evidence, causal diagnosis and source scope

External sources support relevance and mechanisms; they do not prove JejakBalik’s future adoption, cost saving or complete downstream outcome. Keep one concise scope note beside the relevant evidence group instead of a global internal-classification tutorial. The material commercial/operating gap is specific: **no binding operator quotations, institutional-payer commitments or actual partner journey/service logs**. Those inputs are generated through contracted commissioning, category/recipient acceptance and reconciled paid service. The absence is not a reason to restart the adopted idea or turn the essay into a validation plan.

#### 13.1 Latest causal evidence update

The initial source pack had 18 primary sources. The current evidence analyst rechecked four selected sources and added two genuinely new primary sources, so the combined available pack has **20 unique entries**. The two additions are retained for the revised diagnosis. The final bibliography should include only actually cited entries, not every available reference. “Six sources” in the opening brief means a selected opening/diagnosis pack, not six new studies.

Selected opening route: global 2022 generation 62 million tonnes and 22.3% documented formal collection/recycling; Indonesia 1.886 million tonnes/approximately 1.9 Mt in 2022; Java owner reasons; Jakarta information/convenience associations; and the 2024 official policy diagnosis of infrastructure, funding and coordination. Gagasan remains the first named solution introduction.

Updated issue-tree diagnosis uses six causal nodes:

1. Owners lack destination information: Brando household-device 75/305=24.6%.
2. Future use/repair and data decisions remain open: distinguish household later repair 99/305=32.5%; personal backup 118/305=38.69%; personal data 19/305=6.23%.
3. Collection access is not sufficiently usable: Jakarta 2018 convenience–intention r 0.411, information r 0.521; this is association, not a measured pickup delay or causal effect.
4. Collection/processing access is uneven: official Putri 2024 national diagnosis, supported by a geographically bounded informal-actor study; not a new UI facility inventory.
5. Recurring service work lacks sustainable funding: Putri’s official diagnosis plus global treatment cost context, not local willingness-to-pay or a carrier quote.
6. Cross-actor coordination is not sufficiently organized: Putri’s vertical/horizontal diagnosis and Budijati’s city-specific result; accountable handoffs are the team’s operating response, not an observed lost-device percentage.

Journey windows, load, receiver capacity and legal eligibility remain real **design constraints**. They should not be presented as measured Indonesian root causes by themselves. The newer source-backed access/coordination diagnosis improves the problem argument without redesigning matching or removing category screening.

#### 13.2 Numerical/interpretive traps that must survive continuation

- Brando surveyed 403 people in six Java provinces;305 stored devices. Household unknown-destination 75/305 and later repair 99/305 are Table 6; personal data 19/305 is Table 7. Do not use 403 as those denominators, generalize to all Indonesians/UI, merge the two question categories or describe 6.23% as leakage incidence.
- Siringo’s 208 Jakarta respondents were surveyed 2018. r 0.521/r 0.411 are correlations. The 2% formal-facility figure is that historical sample, not a current national formal recycling rate.
- ITU Indonesia’s prose gives 1,886 million kg=1.886 million tonnes, approximately 1.9 Mt. Its compact bullets transpose mass/per-capita values; do not copy them. Regional 0% documented collection is not an Indonesian formal-rate estimate.
- The current official GEM PDF is **second edition,November 2024**. Treatment cost paragraph is printed/physical p. 56; Asia regionalmap with Indonesia rounded 1.9 Mt is printed/physical p. 78. These replace the old p. 54/p 80 locators. The full current PDF is now accessible/downloaded for cited sections, so the old blanket whole-report size-limit statement is superseded there.
- GEM global treatment USD0.36/kg versusUSD0.12/kg across other routes is global context; separate municipal/retailer collection costs are excluded. It is not an Indonesian tariff and is not an input into the financial model.
- Putri’s 2024 policy brief supports its dated national structural diagnosis; do not present it as a verified 2026 facility inventory or import its secondary 17.4% managed statistic without original denominator/method.
- Budijati 2023 surveyed 424 informal used-phone actors across five DIY areas. Infrastructure and organization were significant barriers **only in Kota Yogyakarta**, not the other four areas. Do not call 424 the city subgroup, export the finding to UI or invent coefficients/p-values from an unavailable table.
- Yang 2024 is version 2 of a computational preprint. It supports origin/destination/time/capacity/detour logic; reduced cost may coexist with increased distance. It does not establish this system’s savings or zero emissions.
- Puspita-Sari 2024 author form follows the full PDF’s recommended citation; do not create another identity from inconsistent metadata. Its deterministic profits/33% threshold are not JejakBalik results; do not repeat its outdated “no collection regulation” statement.
- PP22/2021 repealed PP101/2014. Use current category/activity screening through PP27/2020,PP22/2021,Permen LHK6/2021 rather than an older page’s legal reference. The app conveys no transport/processing authority.
- UU27/2022 governs data rights/duties; NIST800-88 Rev 2 September 2025 supersedes Rev 1 and is technical guidance, not Indonesian law. A QR handoff or checkbox is not sanitization evidence. Do not assert a particular DPO threshold without considering the constitutional interpretation noted in the BPK record.
- The HEI count 4,303 covers institutions nationally across ministries, not regional paying clients or campuses. SAM100 is a team offer-coverage assumption, not an official Jabodetabek count.
- Company programme and corporate asset streams are separate. The official Lintasarta factsheet controls facts;380 kg is a programme **target**, not achievement. The corporate 48%repair statistic refers to dismantled devices received at warehouse and returned to production, not all public collection or school donations. Zero NBV is accounting, not zero economic value.
- Do not claim Lintasarta uses AI for disposal, donates retired laptops to schools, operates an e-waste recycling factory, achieved 380 kg, has an unsourced annual volume or approved JejakBalik. Existing programme collaboration with EwasteRJ is not a JejakBalik contract.
- EwasteRJ already advertises pickup, dropbox, reporting and other services. Give those strengths fairly; silence about an integration is not proof that it lacks it or a whole market gap. A public service catalogue is not a verified permit for every category.

#### 13.3 Frameworks and operating controls

Useful selected frameworks remain available: issue tree; verified comparator matrix; PESTEL/PESTLE with team priority weights; IFE/EFE; actual SWOT→TOWS choices; Porter/VRIO only where they add distinct insight; TAM/SAM/SOM; RACI; phased Gantt; risk probability/impact and mitigation owners; SMART outcome definitions; cash flow/BEP/NPV/sensitivity/Monte Carlo. The user suggested frameworks because judges recognize them, while explicitly saying the argument matters more. Do not add more frameworks merely to make the dossier bigger.

Numerical weights/ratings are team judgments, not empirical measurements or external endorsement. Weighted tables must sum their weights and reconcile each rating×weight and total. Risk likelihood/impact 1–5 is ordinal; initial scores and residual targets are not observed probabilities. Existing risk list covers demand/renewal,funding delay,withdrawn journeys,receiver rejection,hazardous conditions,data release,custody mismatch,disputes,exception cost and workload. Each needs trigger, response,one accountable owner and resource. RACI should assign one A per decision; a dispute reviewer must be separate from initial approval even if one person holds several funded roles in a small team.

Environmental reporting should retain distinct physical denominators: requests,assigned,picked up,received,completed,repair returned to owner,reuse placed,materials processed,open holds and mass. One item does not become several new devices because it passes several stages. Planned reporting aligns most directly with SDG12.5, with 12.4/12.6 and 17.17 where actual content fits; alignment is not certification,UN endorsement or an achieved national indicator. Main Dampak now needs quantitative magnitude in addition to service quality; definitions and KPI formulae belong in the dossier.

The following retains the 18 base primary entries and two new revision entries. These public bibliographic names are source authors, not participant identifiers. Use APA7 consistently and include only sources actually cited in the final submission. Official source URLs are durable retrieval leads, not proof that every page has been read; the checked claim scope is stated below. A future new claim requires appropriate source checking, especially for law/company facts.

### 15. Current revision architecture and appendix map

The current revision plan adopts this QR-first mapping (old group → new group): A→B, B→C, C→D, D→E, E→F, F→G, G→H, H→I, I→J, J→K, K→L, L→M. New A is access. Old IDs in the comment ledger describe the annotated source and must be translated when verifying the final artifact.

| Current group | Role |
|---|---|
| A | Access to the public interface demonstration and current source |
| B | Problem and causal evidence |
| C | Alternatives and distinctive configuration |
| D | Participants and stakeholders |
| E | Operating decisions, eligibility, handoffs and holds |
| F | Service interface and actual prototype views |
| G | Authored-scenario comparisons |
| H | Strategic factors and resulting actions |
| I | Market, unit economics, cash and funding |
| J | Commissioning, ten-year horizon and decision accountability |
| K | Risks, response resources and device/data handling |
| L | Quantitative service contribution, outcome chain and metrics |
| M | Reconstructable calculations, distributions and uncertainty |

The plan specifies 10 pt native tables, 12 pt prose, black text, justified descriptive cells, right-aligned numeric cells, no forced break for every appendix, and visible **“Esai”** and **“Daftar lampiran”** returns. Keep at most one short source/interpretation note per exhibit. The completed build consistently uses header fill `#E8EFEA`; `#E4EEE6` in earlier planning notes is obsolete.

Current revised main title and headings:

- Title: **JejakBalik Menyatukan Perjalanan Logistik dan Pemulihan Elektronik Berbasis Institusi**.
- Opening: no premature product explanation.
- Analysis: **Pemulihan terhambat pada akses dan koordinasi**.
- Gagasan: **JejakBalik menyatukan perjalanan dan pemulihan**.
- Finance subchapter: **Pendanaan institusi menjaga keberlanjutan layanan**.
- Implementation subchapter: **Pelaksanaan dimulai dari UI dan Depok**.
- Impact: **Jangkauan pemulihan tumbuh bersama program institusi**.
- Closing: **Kesimpulan**.

The completed-output record controls the current wording and pagination. The durable requirements are the explanatory functions and solution timing. The user’s approximate allocation favours three pages for Gagasan/implementation, three quarters of a page for problem analysis and half a page for impact, inside five measured main pages after opening/closing. Do not force approximate allocations by reducing the required type or spacing.

### 17. Review, verification and continuation rules

The user requires a complete first attempt followed by a rigorous review/revision loop. Define success from the actual request and supplied standards; independently review where separate agents are available; score task fulfilment, correctness, completeness, clarity/usability and presentation/execution 0–10 with observable strengths/defects; verify checkable claims; rank and fix critical/unmet requirements before polish; recheck affected areas. Repeat up to **four rounds**. Stop only when every applicable dimension reaches **8.5** and no known critical error or unmet explicit requirement remains. Do not increase a score merely to pass. Do not claim independence unless a separate review happened. A 9 requires exceptional supporting evidence; 10 is not an ordinary polish score.

Use distinct review roles: skeptical operations/product critic, competition judge/argument reviewer, evidence/citation reviewer, finance/calculation reviewer and design/layout reviewer. The user explicitly requested an additional design agent. Review the actual artifact, not only source scripts. Root owns integration and edits to avoid conflicting mutations. Keep ratings, critique exchanges and assistant process commentary out of the submission. This AI context can retain factual review provenance and limitations without pre-certifying future edits.

For DOCX/PDF, inspect actual rendered pages; measure main-body pages excluding cover/references/appendices; check required fonts, line spacing and margins; inspect every table, figure and screenshot for clipping, gaps, readability and awkward breaks; test real bookmarks/hyperlinks and returns; decode the QR to the owner URL; retain the actual public release finding. A correct LaTeX source or style definition does not prove the delivered file embeds that revision. Inspect the actual embedded image or compare its bytes when claiming a graphic was fixed.

For finance/evidence, reconcile prose, tables and charts to one canonical model. Check cash versus revenue, setup versus operating cost, reserve versus expense, closing institutions versus institution-months, devices versus events, mass versus recovery yield, and scenario comparison boundaries. Every source must support the exact claim/population. Audience citations use meaningful document titles and APA details, never upload filenames, local paths, tool IDs, internal cells or assistant analysis.

Current essay work does not modify repository source. Keep the 17-test rerun and browser captures as bounded observations; identify the 157-test/nine-browser-test release record as historical. New code work would require a separately authorized task, current source inspection and relevant verification.

Material continuation items:

1. Public interface works but is older. Current v 3 source is not established deployed, and this task does not authorize deployment.
2. Cover is reserved for the user. Registration evidence, publication and organizer submission are not completed by file creation.
3. The corpus receipt is limited by the missing Setara remainder, GINTECH printed pages 8–10, broken GoDiet assets and microscopic source-resolution limits. The user cannot provide originals; do not repeatedly block the build on them.
4. Prices, adoption, partner capacity and new mass averages are team assumptions, not agreements or field findings. Preserve one accurate boundary per connected exhibit rather than repeated warnings.
5. Actual final pagination, exhibit/link counts, QR test, embedded selections, bibliography and impact wording are recorded in the completed-output and verification sections. Future material edits require rechecking the affected parts rather than inheriting the old clearance.

Read the completed output record and recover/read the full V5 from its lossless archive before continuing under that manual. Preserve the adopted service and all 49 corrections. Respond to concrete new defects or explicit instructions rather than restarting the project.

## Final essay wording

This is the adopted current prose. DOCX typography, visible hyperlinks and currency spacing are applied by the document producer; this text records the words and their paragraph order. Figures are identified at their actual position.

### JejakBalik Menyatukan Perjalanan Logistik dan Pemulihan Elektronik Berbasis Institusi

Pertumbuhan konsumsi elektronik membawa persoalan yang berlangsung lebih lama daripada masa pakai produknya. Pada 2022, dunia menghasilkan 62 juta ton limbah elektronik, sementara hanya 22,3% massanya tercatat dikumpulkan dan didaur ulang secara formal (Baldé et al., 2024). Perangkat yang keluar dari penggunaan masih menyimpan material bernilai, tetapi sebagian komponennya memerlukan penanganan khusus agar tidak membahayakan manusia dan lingkungan (Putri, 2024). Pemulihan nilai tersebut bergantung pada perjalanan perangkat setelah pemilik berhenti menggunakannya, termasuk keputusan untuk memperbaiki, menggunakan kembali, atau menyerahkannya kepada pengolah yang sesuai.

Di Indonesia, timbulan limbah elektronik mencapai sekitar 1,9 juta ton pada 2022, terbesar di Asia Tenggara pada tahun tersebut (International Telecommunication Union, n.d.). Besarnya arus ini menempatkan pengumpulan sebagai penghubung penting antara konsumsi dan pemulihan. Kajian jaringan pengelolaan di Yogyakarta memperlihatkan perlunya menata hubungan antarpelaku dan kegiatan pengolahan dalam satu jaringan (Puspita-Sari et al., 2024). Dengan demikian, persoalan pemulihan tidak berhenti pada tersedianya teknologi pengolah. Perangkat perlu keluar dari tempat penyimpanan, mencapai penerima yang tepat, dan memperoleh penanganan yang dapat terus dibiayai.

#### Pemulihan terhambat pada akses dan koordinasi

Hambatan pengembalian terbentuk pada keputusan pemilik, akses pengumpulan, dan kesinambungan penyelesaian layanan (Lampiran B.1). Penelitian di enam provinsi Jawa menemukan 305 dari 403 responden menyimpan elektronik bekas atau rusak. Di antara penyimpan tersebut, alasan untuk perangkat rumah tangga meliputi ketidaktahuan mengenai tujuan pembuangan sebesar 24,6% dan keinginan memperbaikinya kelak sebesar 32,5% (Brando et al., 2024). Bagi pemilik, perangkat yang tidak lagi digunakan belum tentu dipandang selesai manfaatnya, sehingga pengumpulan perlu berhadapan dengan alasan penyimpanan tersebut.

Kemudahan penyerahan menjadi persoalan berikutnya. Survei terhadap 208 responden Jakarta pada 2018 menemukan hubungan niat mendaur ulang dengan informasi dan kemudahan, masing-masing berkorelasi 0,521 dan 0,411 (Siringo et al., 2020). Pada tingkat kelembagaan, kajian Bappenas mengidentifikasi keterbatasan fasilitas pengumpulan, persebaran pengolah yang tidak merata, pendanaan, serta koordinasi antarpelaku sebagai hambatan yang saling berkaitan (Putri, 2024). Temuan ini menempatkan akses sebagai bagian dari rangkaian pelayanan, bukan sekadar ketersediaan informasi tempat membuang perangkat (Lampiran B.2).

Saluran pengumpulan sebenarnya telah tersedia. EwasteRJ menawarkan penjemputan, dropbox, dan pelaporan, sedangkan #AksiCintaBumi melalui Lintasarta Peduli Negeri menghubungkan pengumpulan publik dengan pengelolaan perangkat bersama EwasteRJ (EwasteRJ, n.d.; Lintasarta, 2026). Keberadaan layanan tersebut membuka ruang kerja sama, tetapi perluasan akses tetap menghadapi persoalan biaya dan hubungan kerja yang diuraikan di atas. Tantangannya ialah menjadikan pengembalian yang tersebar sebagai layanan berulang dengan tujuan serta tanggung jawab yang tersambung.

#### JejakBalik menyatukan perjalanan dan pemulihan

JejakBalik adalah layanan koordinasi pengembalian perangkat elektronik yang mencocokkan kebutuhan pemilik dengan kapasitas perjalanan yang sudah direncanakan oleh berbagai penyedia logistik. Institusi, seperti kampus atau pengelola komunitas, membiayai program bagi pesertanya. Melalui platform, koordinator mempertemukan permintaan, penawaran perjalanan, dan kesiapan penerima sebelum menyetujui penjemputan. Perangkat kemudian diarahkan pada perbaikan, penggunaan kembali, pemulihan material, atau penanganan khusus sesuai kondisi dan persetujuan pemilik. Setiap perpindahan mempunyai pihak yang bertanggung jawab sampai tindak lanjutnya selesai (Lampiran E.1).

[Gambar 1. Rancangan alur pemulihan JejakBalik. Three faithful cropped 3D scenes: journey network → receiving/inspection facility → device recovery pathway.]

Kontribusinya terletak pada penyatuan kapasitas lintas penyedia dengan pembiayaan dan tanggung jawab pemulihan dalam satu layanan. Operator paket atau penyedia jasa logistik pihak ketiga (3PL), kurir lokal, serta pengangkut barang besar dan khusus menyumbang kemampuan yang berbeda. Perjalanan yang tidak cocok bagi satu perangkat dapat dilayani oleh penawaran lain, sementara institusi memperoleh satu koordinasi program. Penyedia pengumpulan dan pengolah yang sudah ada menjadi bagian jaringan sesuai lingkupnya, sehingga perluasan layanan memanfaatkan hubungan kerja dan perjalanan yang tersedia (Lampiran C.1).

Alurnya dimulai ketika pemilik mencatat perangkat, kondisi, waktu penyerahan, dan tujuan pengembalian. Untuk laptop yang ingin diperbaiki, misalnya, koordinator terlebih dahulu memastikan penerima bersedia menangani perangkat dan instruksi datanya. Penawaran perjalanan kemudian dibandingkan berdasarkan kategori penanganan, muatan, jendela waktu, tambahan durasi, dan anggaran. Pengambilan disisipkan sebelum keberangkatan sambil mempertahankan urutan pekerjaan awal penyedia. Cara ini menggunakan prinsip perjalanan bersama tanpa menganggap seluruh kendaraan yang memiliki ruang pasti dapat menerima penugasan (Yang et al., 2024; Lampiran E.3).

Sesudah rencana disetujui, pemilik dan pembawa mencatat serah terima, lalu penerima mencatat penerimaan dan memeriksa perangkat. Apabila pemeriksaan mengubah jalur yang telah disepakati, keputusan kembali kepada pihak yang berwenang. Pembatalan perjalanan atau persoalan data tidak menghapus tanggung jawab pemegang perangkat terakhir. Koordinator mengatur penyelesaian dan sumber biayanya, sehingga penjemputan membuka rangkaian pemulihan yang terus diikuti sampai kewajiban selesai (Lampiran E.2).

Ilustrasi perencanaan dengan 18 permintaan rekaan menunjukkan nilai penggabungan kapasitas. Pemanfaatan perjalanan tersedia membutuhkan tambahan 28,8 km dan biaya jasa Rp438.700, sedangkan pengumpulan khusus berkelompok memerlukan 112,8 km dan Rp662.400 (Lampiran G.1). Ketika penawaran dibatasi pada satu penyedia, cakupan terbaik menjadi 11 dari 18 permintaan, dibandingkan seluruh permintaan pada jaringan gabungan (Lampiran G.2). Institusi memperoleh jangkauan beberapa jenis penanganan melalui satu program, dengan keputusan penugasan tetap berada pada koordinator.

#### Pendanaan institusi menjaga keberlanjutan layanan

Institusi membayar kesiapan koordinasi dan pekerjaan pemulihan, termasuk ketika perangkat tidak menghasilkan nilai jual. Penawaran menggabungkan biaya koordinasi bulanan dengan tarif perangkat yang diterima tujuan dan direkonsiliasi serah terimanya. Pada estimasi tim, 40 perangkat per bulan menghasilkan tagihan Rp5,48 juta dan biaya langsung Rp1,77 juta bagi satu program. Selisihnya membiayai pengelola, operasi, sistem, dan dukungan bersama (Lampiran I.2). Pembayaran ini memberi penyedia dan penerima imbalan atas pekerjaan yang disepakati, sekaligus menjaga tindak lanjut perangkat yang tertahan.

Bagi institusi, pembelian program menggabungkan akses bagi pesertanya dengan pertanggungjawaban penggunaan anggaran. Lingkup kontrak menentukan kategori, wilayah, waktu layanan, dan bentuk laporan yang dibayar. Institusi dapat melihat perangkat yang sudah diterima, pekerjaan lanjutan, serta biaya kasus yang masih terbuka dalam satu rekonsiliasi (Lampiran C.2). Susunan ini memberi alasan untuk mempertahankan program dari bulan ke bulan: peserta memperoleh akses yang jelas, sementara pembeli mengetahui pekerjaan yang telah dipenuhi dan kewajiban berikutnya. Penerimaan dari penjualan perangkat tetap mengikuti hak pemilik dan kesepakatan tersendiri, sehingga pembiayaan dasar dapat berjalan pada perangkat bernilai rendah sekalipun.

Pada kapasitas tersebut, tiga institusi menutup biaya operasi berulang. Proyeksi 2027–2029 menempatkan pemulihan kas proyek pada bulan ke-22 dan nilai kini bersih (NPV) sebelum pajak sekitar Rp224,67 juta pada diskonto riil 12% (Lampiran I.6). Pendanaan disiapkan bertahap hingga Rp160 juta agar operasi memiliki cadangan ketika perolehan kontrak atau pembayaran bergerak lebih lambat. Batas tersebut melampaui kebutuhan dana persentil ke-90 sekitar Rp155,63 juta dalam simulasi Monte Carlo (Lampiran M.1).

#### Pelaksanaan dimulai dari UI dan Depok

UI/Depok menjadi wilayah pertama untuk menjalankan hubungan institusi pembeli, penyedia perjalanan, penerima, dan pemilik dalam satu program. Dua bulan awal menyiapkan kontrak, petugas, serta catatan bersama. Layanan dimulai pada bulan ketiga, kemudian institusi ditambah pada bulan kelima, ketujuh, dan kesepuluh sesuai kapasitas yang dibiayai (Lampiran J.1). Fasilitas kampus dan ojek pangkalan dapat dilibatkan pada kebutuhan lokal yang sesuai melalui pengaturan akses dan tanggung jawab. Greener Action dari Departemen Lingkungan Hidup BEM UI dapat mendukung edukasi serta pengenalan layanan kepada warga kampus.

Pelaksanaan mengikat perluasan dengan mutu pemenuhan. Matriks tanggung jawab RACI menetapkan penanggung jawab keputusan, sedangkan pengelolaan risiko menghubungkan gangguan perjalanan, penerima, data, dan pembayaran dengan tindakan serta anggaran responsnya (Lampiran J.3; Lampiran K.2). Sebelum program baru dibuka, koordinator menyelaraskan penawaran perjalanan dengan kapasitas penerima dan sumber pembayaran. Permintaan yang belum cocok memperoleh pilihan waktu berikutnya atau pengumpulan khusus yang biayanya disetujui. Perangkat yang sudah berpindah tangan tetap mempunyai pemilik kasus sampai tindak lanjutnya selesai (Lampiran E.4).

Dari UI/Depok, jaringan diperluas ke institusi Jabodetabek dengan hubungan kerja yang mengikuti keadaan setiap wilayah. Rekonsiliasi layanan menjadi dasar pembaruan kontrak dan penambahan program: jumlah perangkat, ketepatan waktu, kewajiban terbuka, serta kas dibaca bersama kapasitas petugas (Lampiran J.4). JejakBalik membawa tata kerja dan catatan yang sama, sementara penyedia perjalanan serta penerima dapat berbeda. Gantt 2027–2036 menempatkan penguatan layanan dan dukungan sebelum replikasi antardaerah, agar pertumbuhan menuju jaringan nasional disertai kemampuan memenuhi janji pada setiap wilayah baru (Lampiran J.2).

#### Jangkauan pemulihan tumbuh bersama program institusi

Proyeksi dasar menempatkan 1.080 perangkat dalam layanan pada 2027 dan 5.040 pada 2029. Selama tiga tahun, cakupannya mencapai 9.240 perangkat, dengan rentang 4.505–11.180 pada skenario pertumbuhan rendah hingga tinggi (Lampiran L.1). Pada volume dasar, rentang bobot kategori yang diestimasi tim setara dengan 29,6–82,7 ton perangkat menuju penerima terdokumentasi. Perbaikan, penempatan guna ulang, dan hasil pengolahan kemudian dicatat sesuai jalurnya untuk menunjukkan kontribusi pada pengurangan sampah melalui guna ulang dan daur ulang dalam SDG 12.5 (United Nations Department of Economic and Social Affairs, n.d.-a; Lampiran L.4).

Manfaat ekonomi menjangkau pelaksana layanan melalui 9.000 jam kerja pengelola dan operasi yang dianggarkan selama tiga tahun, sekitar Rp201 juta untuk angkutan, serta Rp162,6 juta untuk penanganan penerima (Lampiran L.1). Pendanaan tersebut mengakui pekerjaan yang diperlukan untuk menjaga arus pemulihan. Target sekurang-kurangnya 95% layanan tepat waktu kemudian menjaga agar pertumbuhan jangkauan tetap disertai pemenuhan janji penjemputan dan penerimaan (Lampiran L.3).

#### Kesimpulan

Hambatan pemulihan elektronik muncul ketika keputusan pemilik, akses pengumpulan, pembiayaan, dan tanggung jawab berjalan terpisah. JejakBalik menyatukannya melalui pencocokan perjalanan lintas penyedia, program institusi berbayar, serta serah terima yang terus diikuti sampai penyelesaian. Proyeksi jangkauan dan pembiayaannya menunjukkan skala kontribusi yang dapat dibangun dari program UI/Depok menuju jaringan antarwilayah. Dengan susunan tersebut, perluasan pengembalian perangkat memperoleh jalur angkut, tujuan penerimaan, dan pekerjaan pemulihan yang dapat dipertahankan bersama.

## Durable source and asset recovery

These are retrieval identities, not audience-facing citations. Persistent identifiers are exact; if bytes are unavailable in a future environment, do not invent a read.
